## Part 0. Vol.3 안내

### 0.1 Vol.3의 역할

Vol.1은 출장 동선의 기본 장면, Vol.2는 심화·전문 장면을 다뤘다. Vol.3는 두 권에서 비어 있던 **'대화를 운영하는 기술'**과 **'전문가 역할의 다양화'**를 채운다.

공통 영역은 일상 기능 표현을 정교하게 나눴다. 부탁의 크기 조절, 시간·약속, 묘사, 비교·선택, 계획·결정, 확신의 정도, 습관·익숙함, 메신저 구어, 공손도 조절이 여기에 속한다. 특히 PLT(공손도 조절)는 같은 의미를 친구·정중·매우 정중의 세 온도로 나란히 보여 준다. 상황에 맞는 격식을 고르는 연습에 쓰면 된다.

여행 영역은 Vol.1·2에서 다루지 않은 장면을 보강했다. 라운지·우수회원, 호스텔, 식이 요구 상세 대화, 약사 상담, 안내방송·표지 독해, 인접국(프랑스·체코·스위스·영국) 이동, 만성질환·당뇨 여행 관리, 야간열차, 장기 체류, 대사관 영사 서비스, 숙소 오버부킹 대응 등이다.

학회·연구 영역은 본인의 전문 분야를 더 깊이 다룬다. 전문 분야로는 방사선 생물·역학, 몬테카를로, 내부피폭, 환경감시·크리깅, 비상대응, 의료방사선, 측정 불확도, 품질보증, 교정 실무, 규제 검사 대응, 원전 정비 맥락, ICRP 방호 체계가 들어갔다. 연구 수행 측면에서는 NLP, LLM 평가, 온프렘 인프라, 에이전트, 데이터 파이프라인, 실험 설계, 재현 연구를 다뤘다. 연구 운영 측면에서는 면접, 공동 집필, 비판적 토론, 보고서, 스토리텔링, 킥오프 회의, 상사 보고, 브레인스토밍, 특허, 학생 지도, 적대적 질문 대응을 담았다.

### 0.2 중복 배제 방법

Vol.2와 같은 정규화 비교를 Vol.1·Vol.2 전체(6,111개)를 대상으로 수행했다. 정규화는 NFKC → 소문자 → 따옴표 통일 → 구두점 제거 → 공백 압축 순서로 진행했다. Vol.3 내부 중복도 같은 기준으로 제거했다.

Vol.3 빌드 스크립트는 Vol.2의 중복 제거 과정을 그대로 재현해 Vol.2 ID를 다시 부여한다. 그 결과 Vol.1·Vol.2의 ID는 이전 배포본과 동일하게 유지된다. 이 동일성은 Vol.2 채택 수 3,019개가 일치하는지로 검증했다.

### 0.3 신규 패턴 10종의 쓰임

Vol.3에서 추가한 패턴 중 학회 영어의 수준을 한 단계 올리는 것은 세 가지다.

- **F22 *To what extent ___?***: yes/no 대신 정도를 물어 토론을 연다.
- **F21 *Assuming ___***: 분석의 전제를 먼저 밝혀 반론의 여지를 줄인다.
- **G12 *To put this in context, ___***: 수치를 청중이 체감할 수 있는 기준과 연결한다.

H09 *Let me get back to you on ___*는 모르는 질문에 정직하게 답하면서 신뢰를 지키는 문형이다. 규제 검사, 상사 보고, 공동 집필에서 반복적으로 쓰인다. C17 *I'm (not) used to ___*는 *used to + 동사원형*(과거 습관)과 혼동하기 쉬우니 함께 대조해 익힌다.

### 0.4 학습 경로 연계

권장 순서는 Vol.1 Part 1 → Vol.2 Part 1 → Vol.3 Part 1이다. 이렇게 하면 공통 기능 표현이 세 겹으로 쌓인다.

그다음은 역할에 따라 고른다.

- **발표자**: Vol.3의 STY·NRV·HOS·CRI를 먼저 본다.
- **전문 토론**: 본인 분야 카테고리(BIO·MCS·IPX·UNC·LEV 등)를 본다.
- **협력 운영**: KOM·CWR·TPR·BOS를 본다.

Vol.3 Part 2는 Vol.2처럼 참고서로 쓴다. 출발 전에 예상되는 상황의 카테고리만 골라 읽으면 된다.

## 신규 패턴 10종

Vol.1·2의 129개 패턴으로 포착되지 않던 기능을 보완하기 위해 추가한 패턴이다.

| ID | 패턴 | 기능 | 사용 메모 | Vol.2 수록 |
|---|---|---|---|---:|
| A17 | **Is there any way ___?** | 간절한 가능성 타진 | 규정상 어려운 일을 부탁할 때. Would it be possible보다 절박함이 묻어난다. | 6 |
| B19 | **How often ___?** | 빈도 묻기 | 교통 배차, 회의 주기, 측정 주기 등. | 11 |
| B20 | **How far ___? / How close ___?** | 거리·근접 정도 묻기 | 도보 거리, 숙소와 회의장 간격 등. | 7 |
| C17 | **I'm (not) used to ___** | 익숙함·적응 표현 | be used to + 명사/동명사. used to + 동사원형(과거 습관)과 구분해야 한다. | 12 |
| E16 | **I can't wait to ___ / I'm looking forward to ___** | 기대 표현 | looking forward to 뒤에는 동명사. | 6 |
| F21 | **Assuming (that) ___** | 가정 제시 | 분석의 전제를 먼저 밝혀 반론의 여지를 줄인다. | 14 |
| F22 | **To what extent ___?** | 정도 질문 | yes/no 대신 정도를 물어 토론을 연다. 학회 질문의 고급 문형. | 12 |
| G12 | **To put this in context, ___** | 맥락화 | 수치를 청중이 체감할 기준과 연결한다. | 11 |
| H09 | **Let me get back to you on ___** | 답변 보류·후속 약속 | 즉답 대신 정확성을 택할 때. | 6 |
| K10 | **Just a quick note to ___** | 짧은 메일 도입 | 가벼운 공지·감사·확인 메일의 첫 문장. | 6 |

## Part 1. 공통 의사소통 확장

### `U-FVR` 부탁하기 — 크고 작은 부탁

*상황 40개 · ★ 1개*


**A02 · Could you ___ (for me)?** — 상대에게 행동을 부탁

- **Could you mind my things while I grab a coffee?** — 커피 사는 동안 짐 `U-FVR-001`
- **Could you pick up a bottle of water for me on your way?** — 오는 길에 물 `U-FVR-002`
- **Could you help me understand what this letter says?** — 편지 해석 `U-FVR-003`
- **Could you call them for me? My German isn't good enough.** — 대신 전화 `U-FVR-004`
- **Could you send me a photo of the schedule?** — 시간표 사진 `U-FVR-005`
- **Could you swap places with me so I can sit near the door?** — 문 가까운 자리 `U-FVR-006`
- **Could you write a short recommendation for me?** — 짧은 추천 `U-FVR-007`
- **Could you put me in touch with someone at the embassy?** — 대사관 연결 `U-FVR-008`
- **Could you wait two minutes? I'm almost ready.** — 2분 대기 `U-FVR-009`
- **Could you give me a heads-up if the plan changes?** — 변경 시 귀띔 `U-FVR-010`
- **Could you sign for a package if it arrives while I'm out?** — 택배 대리 서명 `U-FVR-011`

**A03 · Would you mind ___ing?** — 부담이 있는 부탁

- **Would you mind lending me ten euros? I'll pay you back tonight.** — 10유로 빌리기 `U-FVR-012`
- **Would you mind sharing your umbrella as far as the station?** — 우산 같이 쓰기 `U-FVR-013`
- **Would you mind lending me your laptop for five minutes?** — 노트북 빌리기 `U-FVR-014`
- **Would you mind holding the door for a second?** — 문 잡아주기 `U-FVR-015`
- **Would you mind keeping it down a little? I'm on a call.** — 통화 중 조용히 `U-FVR-016`
- **Would you mind going over that one more time?** — 한 번 더 설명 `U-FVR-017`

**A07 · I was wondering if ___** — 매우 조심스러운 부탁

- **I hate to ask, but could you lend me your charger for an hour?** — 큰 부탁 도입 `U-FVR-018`

**A13 · Could you help me with ___?** — 도움 요청

- **Could you help me with this registration form?** — 등록 양식 도움 `U-FVR-019`
- **Could you help me with setting up this app?** — 앱 설정 도움 `U-FVR-020`
- **Could you help me with this box? It's heavier than it looks.** — 무거운 상자 `U-FVR-021`

**A17 · Is there any way ___?** — 간절한 가능성 타진

- ★ **Is there any way you could fit me in today?** — 가능성 타진 `U-FVR-022`
- **Is there any way to get this done by Friday?** — 금요일까지 간청 `U-FVR-023`
- **Is there any way we could sit together?** — 나란히 앉기 간청 `U-FVR-024`
- **Is there any way around this rule?** — 규정 우회 `U-FVR-025`
- **Is there any way to speed this up?** — 처리 가속 `U-FVR-026`
- **Is there any way I could still get a refund?** — 환불 간청 `U-FVR-027`

**Z00 · (고정 표현 · chunk)** — 패턴으로 쪼개지 않고 통째로 외우는 관용 덩어리

- **I have a big favor to ask.** — 큰 부탁 예고 `U-FVR-028`
- **Could I ask you a small favor?** — 작은 부탁 예고 `U-FVR-029`
- **No pressure at all — only if you have time.** — 부담 줄이기 `U-FVR-030`
- **Feel free to say no.** — 거절해도 됨 `U-FVR-031`
- **I wouldn't ask if it weren't urgent.** — 급한 사정 `U-FVR-032`
- **Of course — happy to.** — 기꺼이 수락 `U-FVR-033`
- **Sure, as long as I get it back by six.** — 조건부 수락 (반납) `U-FVR-034`
- **I'm really sorry, but I can't today.** — 미안한 거절 `U-FVR-035`
- **I can't do that, but I know someone who might.** — 대안 인물 `U-FVR-036`
- **You're a lifesaver.** — 구세주 `U-FVR-037`
- **I'll return the favor sometime.** — 보답 약속 `U-FVR-038`
- **Are you sure it's not too much trouble?** — 부담 확인 `U-FVR-039`
- **Just a gentle reminder about that favor I asked.** — 부탁 상기 `U-FVR-040`

### `U-TIM` 시간·약속 표현

*상황 40개 · ★ 2개*


**B19 · How often ___?** — 빈도 묻기

- ★ **How often do the trains run at night?** — 야간 배차 `U-TIM-001`
- **How often does the working group meet?** — 워킹그룹 주기 `U-TIM-002`

## Part 2. 여행 확장

### `T-MIL` 항공 마일리지·라운지·우수회원

*상황 35개 · ★ 1개*


**A01 · Could I get ___? / Can I have ___?** — 물건·서비스 요청 (가장 범용)

- **Could I get a sparkling water, please?** — 라운지 탄산수 `T-MIL-001`

**A02 · Could you ___ (for me)?** — 상대에게 행동을 부탁

- **Could you attach my membership number to the reservation?** — 회원번호 연결 `T-MIL-002`

**A04 · I'd like (to) ___** — 원하는 것을 정중히 밝힘

- **I'd like to put my name down for a shower.** — 샤워 대기 등록 `T-MIL-003`

**A06 · Would it be possible to ___? / Is it possible to ___?** — 규정 밖·예외 요청

- **Is it possible to use the priority line with my status?** — 우선 줄 이용 `T-MIL-004`

**A09 · Can I ___? / Is it okay if I ___?** — 허락 구하기

- **Can my colleague come in with me?** — 동료 동반 입장 `T-MIL-005`

**B03 · Is there ___ (around here)?** — 존재 여부

- **Is there a nap room in the lounge?** — 라운지 수면실 `T-MIL-006`
- **Is there a waiting list for the showers?** — 샤워 대기 명단 `T-MIL-007`
- **Is there a printer I can use in the lounge?** — 라운지 프린터 `T-MIL-008`
- **Is there another lounge that's less busy?** — 덜 붐비는 라운지 `T-MIL-009`

**B04 · Do you have ___?** — 보유·재고 여부

- **Do you have any English newspapers?** — 영어 신문 `T-MIL-010`

**B05 · What time ___? / When ___?** — 시각·시점

- **What time does the lounge open in the morning?** — 라운지 아침 개장 `T-MIL-011`
- **When do my miles expire?** — 마일 만료 `T-MIL-012`
- **What time do you serve hot food?** — 따뜻한 음식 시간 `T-MIL-013`

**B06 · How long ___?** — 소요 시간·기간

- **How long does it take for the miles to show up?** — 적립 반영 기간 `T-MIL-014`
- **How long does it take to walk from here to the gate?** — 라운지→게이트 `T-MIL-015`

**B07 · How much ___?** — 가격·양

- **How much is it for a guest?** — 동반자 요금 `T-MIL-016`

**B09 · Is this ___? / Does this ___?** — 예·아니오 확인

- **Do you announce boarding in the lounge?** — 라운지 탑승 방송 `T-MIL-017`
- **Is this lounge open to Star Alliance Gold members?** — 스타얼라이언스 골드 `T-MIL-018`
- **Am I on the upgrade list?** — 업그레이드 명단 `T-MIL-019`
- **Can I transfer miles to a family member?** — 마일 양도 `T-MIL-020`
- **Can I use miles to pay for extra baggage?** — 마일로 수하물 `T-MIL-021`
- **Is there hot food, or just snacks?** — 따뜻한 음식 여부 `T-MIL-022`

**B10 · Do I need to ___? / Do I have to ___?** — 의무·필요 여부

- **Do I need the boarding pass to claim missing miles?** — 사후 적립 증빙 `T-MIL-023`
- **Do you need to see my membership card?** — 회원카드 제시 `T-MIL-024`

**B11 · Is ___ included? / Does it come with ___?** — 포함 여부

- **Does my status include a guest in the lounge?** — 라운지 동반 포함 `T-MIL-025`
- **Does my status give me an extra bag allowance?** — 등급 수하물 추가 `T-MIL-026`

**B15 · What's the ___?** — 정보 항목 묻기

- **What are the benefits at this tier?** — 등급 혜택 `T-MIL-027`
- **What number am I on the upgrade list?** — 업그레이드 순번 `T-MIL-028`

**C05 · I think there's a mistake ___** — 오류 지적 (완곡)

- **I think my status isn't showing on this booking.** — 등급 미반영 `T-MIL-029`

**C14 · I was told ___** — 근거 제시

- **I was told my status would be valid until March.** — 등급 유효기간 `T-MIL-030`

**Z00 · (고정 표현 · chunk)** — 패턴으로 쪼개지 않고 통째로 외우는 관용 덩어리

- ★ **I don't think my miles from the last flight were credited.** — 마일 미적립 `T-MIL-031`
- **Is it all right if I sit here?** — 라운지 착석 허락 `T-MIL-032`
- **My card is in the app — here.** — 앱 회원카드 `T-MIL-033`
- **Oh, wonderful — thank you so much!** — 업그레이드 성공 `T-MIL-034`
- **It's really crowded in here today.** — 라운지 혼잡 `T-MIL-035`

## Part 3. 학회·연구 활동 확장

### `C-BIO` 방사선 생물학적 영향·역학

*상황 51개 · ★ 3개*


**D04 · What does ___ mean? / What do you mean by ___?** — 의미 확인

- **Sorry, ERR here means excess relative risk?** — ERR 약어 `C-BIO-001`

**F05 · The results suggest ___ / It seems that ___** — 헤징 (단정 회피)

- **It seems the excess risk is consistent with LNT, but the confidence intervals are wide.** — LNT 부합 헤징 `C-BIO-002`
- **These findings suggest an association, but causality is hard to establish.** — 인과 신중 `C-BIO-003`

**F11 · I see what you mean, but ___** — 정중한 반대

- **I see your point, but the absence of evidence isn't evidence of absence.** — 증거 부재 ≠ 부재 증거 `C-BIO-004`

**F13 · How did you ___?** — 방법 질문

- ★ **How did you reconstruct the doses for the early years?** — 초기 선량 재구성 `C-BIO-005`
- **How did you control for smoking?** — 흡연 통제 `C-BIO-006`
- **How did you choose the lag period?** — 지연기간 선택 `C-BIO-007`

**F14 · Did you consider ___? / Have you tried ___?** — 대안 제시형 질문

- **Did you adjust for the healthy worker effect?** — 건강노동자 보정 `C-BIO-008`
- **Did you look at circulatory diseases as well?** — 순환기 포함 `C-BIO-009`

**F18 · It depends on ___** — 조건부 답

- **It depends on age at exposure and sex.** — 연령·성별 의존 `C-BIO-010`

**F20 · In terms of ___** — 관점 한정

- **In terms of public health, the risk from radon is much more important.** — 공중보건 측면 라돈 `C-BIO-011`

**F22 · To what extent ___?** — 정도 질문

- **To what extent do you think the healthy worker effect explains the results?** — 건강노동자 효과 정도 `C-BIO-012`
- **To what extent can we transfer risk estimates from Japan to Korea?** — 위험 전이 정도 `C-BIO-013`

**G12 · To put this in context, ___** — 맥락화

- ★ **To put this in context, the lifetime cancer risk from 1 millisievert is about one in 20,000.** — 1mSv 위험 맥락 `C-BIO-014`
- **To put this in context, about 40 percent of people will develop cancer anyway.** — 자연 발생 암 비교 `C-BIO-015`
- **To put this in context, smoking a pack a day carries a far higher risk.** — 흡연 비교 `C-BIO-016`

**Z00 · (고정 표현 · chunk)** — 패턴으로 쪼개지 않고 통째로 외우는 관용 덩어리

- ★ **The atomic bomb survivor cohort is still the main basis for risk estimates.** — 원폭 생존자 코호트 `C-BIO-017`
- **The nominal risk coefficient is about five percent per sievert for the whole population.** — 명목 위험계수 `C-BIO-018`
- **At low doses, the epidemiological evidence is much weaker.** — 저선량 증거 약함 `C-BIO-019`
- **Below about 100 millisieverts, studies don't have the power to detect an effect.** — 100mSv 이하 검정력 `C-BIO-020`
- **The INWORKS study pooled data from nuclear workers in France, the UK, and the US.** — INWORKS 소개 `C-BIO-021`
- **It found a small but significant increase in solid cancer risk.** — INWORKS 결과 `C-BIO-022`
- **We have to account for the healthy worker effect.** — 건강노동자 효과 `C-BIO-023`
- **Smoking is a major confounder in lung cancer studies.** — 흡연 교란 `C-BIO-024`
- **There's growing interest in circulatory disease at moderate doses.** — 순환기 질환 `C-BIO-025`
- **Lens opacities appear at lower doses than we used to think.** — 수정체 혼탁 저선량 `C-BIO-026`
- **ICRP now considers the threshold for cataracts to be around 0.5 gray.** — 백내장 역치 0.5Gy `C-BIO-027`
- **Children are much more sensitive to thyroid cancer from radioiodine.** — 소아 갑상선 민감 `C-BIO-028`
- **The increase in thyroid cancer after Chernobyl was mostly in people exposed as children.** — 체르노빌 갑상선암 `C-BIO-029`
- **The Fukushima thyroid screening found many cases, partly because of intensive screening.** — 후쿠시마 검진 효과 `C-BIO-030`
- **Overdiagnosis is a real issue in thyroid screening.** — 과잉진단 `C-BIO-031`
- **ICRP uses a dose and dose-rate effectiveness factor of two.** — DDREF 2 `C-BIO-032`
- **Some argue the DDREF should be dropped altogether.** — DDREF 폐지론 `C-BIO-033`
- **Hereditary effects haven't been observed in humans, only in animals.** — 유전 영향 `C-BIO-034`
- **Tissue weighting factors reflect the relative sensitivity of different organs.** — 조직가중계수 `C-BIO-035`
- **Neutrons have a higher radiation weighting factor than photons.** — 중성자 가중계수 `C-BIO-036`
- **Tissue reactions have a threshold; stochastic effects are assumed not to.** — 조직반응 vs 확률적 `C-BIO-037`
- **Effective dose isn't meant for estimating individual risk.** — 유효선량 개인 위험 부적합 `C-BIO-038`
- **For compensation cases, we calculate the probability of causation.** — 기인확률 계산 `C-BIO-039`
- **It's the probability that a given cancer was caused by the radiation exposure.** — 기인확률 정의 `C-BIO-040`
- **Solid cancers usually appear ten years or more after exposure.** — 고형암 잠복기 `C-BIO-041`
- **Leukemia has a shorter latency, around two to five years.** — 백혈병 잠복기 `C-BIO-042`
- **Transferring risk from Japan to other populations is a big source of uncertainty.** — 위험 전이 불확실 `C-BIO-043`
- **We used an excess relative risk model.** — ERR 모형 `C-BIO-044`
- **The dose-response curve looks linear in this range.** — 선형 범위 `C-BIO-045`
- **Second cancers after radiotherapy are an important data source.** — 방사선치료 이차암 `C-BIO-046`
- **The CT cohorts gave new data on childhood exposure.** — CT 코호트 `C-BIO-047`
- **In the CT studies, reverse causation was a concern.** — CT 역인과 우려 `C-BIO-048`
- **At the cellular level, DNA double-strand breaks are the key lesion.** — DNA 이중가닥 절단 `C-BIO-049`
- **Dicentric chromosomes are still the gold standard for biodosimetry.** — 이동원 염색체 `C-BIO-050`
- **Biological dosimetry can confirm whether a high exposure really happened.** — 생물학적 선량평가 역할 `C-BIO-051`

### `C-MCS` 몬테카를로·계산 선량평가

*상황 42개 · ★ 3개*


**D04 · What does ___ mean? / What do you mean by ___?** — 의미 확인

- **By "F6 tally," you mean energy deposition?** — F6 탤리 `C-MCS-001`

**F05 · The results suggest ___ / It seems that ___** — 헤징 (단정 회피)

- **It seems the geometry matters more than the code itself.** — 기하가 코드보다 중요 `C-MCS-002`

**F07 · One limitation is ___** — 한계 인정

- **One limitation is that we used a single phantom size.** — 단일 체격 한계 `C-MCS-003`

**F13 · How did you ___?** — 방법 질문

- ★ **Which variance reduction techniques did you use?** — 분산감소 기법 `C-MCS-004`
- **How did you validate the model?** — 모델 검증 방법 `C-MCS-005`
- **How did you define the material composition?** — 재질 정의 `C-MCS-006`

**F14 · Did you consider ___? / Have you tried ___?** — 대안 제시형 질문

- **Have you tried the mesh-type phantoms?** — 메시 팬텀 시도 `C-MCS-007`
- **Did you compare MCNP with Geant4?** — 코드 간 비교 `C-MCS-008`

**F17 · This is based on ___** — 근거·출처

- **The voxel phantoms are based on ICRP Publication 110.** — ICRP 110 복셀 `C-MCS-009`

**F21 · Assuming (that) ___** — 가정 제시

- ★ **Assuming the worker is facing the source, the dose would be about 20 percent higher.** — 선원 정면 가정 `C-MCS-010`
- **Assuming an isotropic field, the conversion coefficient drops quite a bit.** — 등방장 가정 `C-MCS-011`
- **Assuming the spectrum doesn't change, we can just scale the results.** — 스펙트럼 불변 가정 `C-MCS-012`

**Z00 · (고정 표현 · chunk)** — 패턴으로 쪼개지 않고 통째로 외우는 관용 덩어리

- ★ **We used MCNP6 for the transport calculations.** — MCNP6 `C-MCS-013`
- **For the detector response, we used Geant4.** — Geant4 `C-MCS-014`
- **PHITS is popular in Japan and Korea for this kind of work.** — PHITS `C-MCS-015`
- **We used the ICRP reference computational phantoms.** — ICRP 기준 팬텀 `C-MCS-016`
- **The new mesh-type phantoms are much more realistic.** — 메시형 팬텀 `C-MCS-017`
- **We ran ten billion histories.** — 100억 이력 `C-MCS-018`
- **The statistical uncertainty was below one percent.** — 통계오차 1% 미만 `C-MCS-019`
- **We used variance reduction to speed up the calculation.** — 분산 감소 `C-MCS-020`
- **Each run took about 12 hours on our cluster.** — 클러스터 12시간 `C-MCS-021`
- **The source term is based on the measured spectrum.** — 선원항 `C-MCS-022`
- **We used a realistic energy spectrum instead of a single energy.** — 실제 스펙트럼 `C-MCS-023`
- **We simulated AP, PA, lateral, and isotropic geometries.** — 조사 기하 `C-MCS-024`
- **We calculated fluence-to-dose conversion coefficients.** — 변환계수 계산 `C-MCS-025`
- **Our results agree with ICRP Publication 116 within a few percent.** — ICRP 116 일치 `C-MCS-026`
- **We modeled the dosimeter card in detail, including the filters.** — 선량계 카드 모델링 `C-MCS-027`
- **The filters change the energy response quite a lot below 100 keV.** — 필터 효과 `C-MCS-028`
- **We validated the model against measurements in reference fields.** — 기준장 검증 `C-MCS-029`
- **Most of the differences come from the material composition.** — 재질 조성 차이 `C-MCS-030`
- **We used ENDF/B-VIII cross-section data.** — ENDF/B-VIII `C-MCS-031`
- **Neutron transport is where most of the computing time goes.** — 중성자 계산시간 `C-MCS-032`
- **We tracked secondary electrons down to 10 keV.** — 2차 전자 추적 `C-MCS-033`
- **We assumed charged-particle equilibrium in the kerma approximation.** — 커마 근사 `C-MCS-034`
- **The kerma approximation breaks down at high energies.** — 커마 근사 한계 `C-MCS-035`
- **For skin dose, we scored the energy deposited at 70 micrometers.** — 피부 70μm `C-MCS-036`
- **For the lens, we used a detailed eye model.** — 상세 눈 모델 `C-MCS-037`
- **We trained a neural network as a surrogate for the Monte Carlo code.** — 신경망 대리모델 `C-MCS-038`
- **The surrogate is about a thousand times faster.** — 대리모델 속도 `C-MCS-039`
- **The surrogate error is within two percent of the full simulation.** — 대리모델 오차 `C-MCS-040`
- **We ran the jobs in parallel on 64 cores.** — 64코어 병렬 `C-MCS-041`
- **We're happy to share the input files.** — 입력파일 공유 `C-MCS-042`

### `C-IPX` 내부피폭 심화

*상황 36개 · ★ 2개*

- **Sorry, AMAD is the activity median aerodynamic diameter, right?** — AMAD 확인 `C-IPX-001`

**F07 · One limitation is ___** — 한계 인정

- **One limitation is the lack of urine data for most workers.** — 소변 자료 부족 `C-IPX-003`

- ★ **How did you handle the unknown intake date?** — 섭취 시점 처리 `C-IPX-004`
- **How do you correct for body size?** — 체격 보정 방법 `C-IPX-005`
- **How do you decide whether a peak is real?** — 피크 판정 `C-IPX-006`

**F14 · Did you consider ___? / Have you tried ___?** — 대안 제시형 질문

- **Did you consider individual-specific parameters?** — 개인 맞춤 매개변수 `C-IPX-008`

- **In our case, routine monitoring is done with NaI detectors.** — NaI 정기 감시 `C-IPX-009`

**F21 · Assuming (that) ___** — 가정 제시

- **Assuming a single acute intake, the estimate is about 0.3 millisieverts.** — 단일 급성 섭취 가정 `C-IPX-010`

**Z00 · (고정 표현 · chunk)** — 패턴으로 쪼개지 않고 통째로 외우는 관용 덩어리

- ★ **We used the ICRP biokinetic models for cesium and cobalt.** — 생체동역학 모형 `C-IPX-011`
- **The Occupational Intakes of Radionuclides series replaced the older models.** — OIR 시리즈 `C-IPX-012`
- **The human respiratory tract model is the starting point for inhalation.** — 호흡기 모형 `C-IPX-013`
- **We assumed an AMAD of five micrometers.** — AMAD 5μm `C-IPX-014`
- **We used absorption type M for this compound.** — 흡수유형 M `C-IPX-015`
- **When the intake date is unknown, we assume it happened at the midpoint.** — 섭취 시점 중간 가정 `C-IPX-016`
- **Workers are measured every three months.** — 3개월 측정 `C-IPX-017`
- **For tritium, we use urine samples.** — 삼중수소 소변 `C-IPX-018`
- **The samples were analyzed by liquid scintillation counting.** — 액체섬광계수 `C-IPX-019`
- **The counter uses a chair geometry.** — 의자형 기하 `C-IPX-020`
- **We calibrate with a BOMAB phantom.** — BOMAB 교정 `C-IPX-021`
- **We correct for body size using height and weight.** — 체격 보정 `C-IPX-022`
- **Some peaks overlap, which makes identification tricky.** — 피크 중첩 `C-IPX-023`
- **Optimizing the nuclide library reduced false identifications.** — 라이브러리 최적화 효과 `C-IPX-024`
- **False positives cause unnecessary follow-up measurements.** — 위양성 부담 `C-IPX-025`
- **Potassium-40 is always there in the spectrum.** — K-40 `C-IPX-026`
- **We have to rule out external contamination before assuming an intake.** — 외부오염 배제 `C-IPX-027`
- **We remeasure after the worker showers.** — 샤워 후 재측정 `C-IPX-028`
- **The committed effective dose was well below one millisievert.** — 예탁선량 1mSv 미만 `C-IPX-029`
- **We used IMBA for the dose assessment.** — IMBA `C-IPX-030`
- **The uncertainty in internal dose is usually much larger than for external dose.** — 내부 불확도 큼 `C-IPX-031`
- **Below the recording level, we don't calculate a dose.** — 내부 기록준위 `C-IPX-032`
- **In an accident, early measurements are critical.** — 사고 초기 측정 `C-IPX-033`
- **Prussian blue can speed up cesium excretion.** — 프러시안 블루 `C-IPX-034`
- **Stable iodine blocks thyroid uptake if taken early enough.** — 안정 요오드 `C-IPX-035`
- **We also do thyroid counting for iodine.** — 갑상선 계측 `C-IPX-036`

- **Most alarms turn out to be caused by rain.** — 비가 오경보 원인 `C-ENV-016`
- **Kriging gives us both an estimate and its uncertainty at unmeasured points.** — 크리깅 장점 `C-ENV-017`
- **The variogram shows spatial correlation up to about two kilometers.** — 베리오그램 2km `C-ENV-018`
- **There's a noticeable nugget effect, probably from measurement noise.** — 너겟 효과 `C-ENV-019`
- **We found some anisotropy along the valley.** — 계곡 방향 이방성 `C-ENV-020`
- **We used leave-one-out cross-validation for the interpolation.** — LOO 교차검증 `C-ENV-021`

- **Drones are becoming useful for mapping hot spots.** — 드론 핫스팟 `C-ENV-023`
- **We collect soil samples once a year.** — 연 1회 토양 `C-ENV-024`
- **Seawater and fish are sampled near the outfall.** — 해양 시료 `C-ENV-025`
- **Tritium releases are well below the limit.** — 삼중수소 방출 한도 `C-ENV-026`
- **The estimated dose to the public is a few microsieverts per year.** — 주민 선량 `C-ENV-027`
- **We use an atmospheric dispersion model to estimate public dose.** — 대기확산 모형 `C-ENV-028`
- **Meteorological data are recorded on site.** — 현장 기상 `C-ENV-029`
- **All monitoring data are published online in real time.** — 실시간 공개 `C-ENV-030`
- **Short gaps happen when a station loses power.** — 정전 결측 `C-ENV-031`
- **We're testing machine learning to flag unusual readings.** — ML 이상 탐지 `C-ENV-032`

- **Protective actions include sheltering, evacuation, and iodine prophylaxis.** — 보호조치 종류 `C-EMG-010`
- **The reference level in an emergency is between 20 and 100 millisieverts.** — 비상 참조준위 `C-EMG-011`
- **Emergency workers may receive higher doses to save lives.** — 긴급작업자 `C-EMG-012`
- **Doses above 100 millisieverts should only be accepted by volunteers.** — 자원자 원칙 `C-EMG-013`
- **In the first hours, we rely on models rather than measurements.** — 초기 모델 의존 `C-EMG-014`
- **As soon as possible, we switch to measured data.** — 측정 전환 `C-EMG-015`
- **Decision support tools help the authorities choose protective actions.** — 의사결정 지원 `C-EMG-016`
- **Communication with the public is just as important as the technical response.** — 대중 소통 중요 `C-EMG-017`
- **Clear, consistent messages reduce panic.** — 일관된 메시지 `C-EMG-018`
- **We run a full-scale exercise every year.** — 연 1회 종합훈련 `C-EMG-019`
- **We're using language models to generate more varied exercise scenarios.** — LLM 시나리오 생성 `C-EMG-020`
- **After each drill, we hold a debriefing session.** — 훈련 후 디브리핑 `C-EMG-021`
- **One lesson from Fukushima was the importance of mental health support.** — 후쿠시마 정신건강 교훈 `C-EMG-022`
- **The criteria for returning home were controversial.** — 귀환 기준 논란 `C-EMG-023`
- **Long-term recovery is often harder than the emergency phase.** — 장기 복구 어려움 `C-EMG-024`
- **People leaving the zone were screened for contamination.** — 오염 검사 `C-EMG-025`
- **Decontamination was largely effective in urban areas.** — 도심 제염 `C-EMG-026`
- **Food restrictions were based on Codex guideline levels.** — 식품 기준 `C-EMG-027`

**Z00 · (고정 표현 · chunk)** — 패턴으로 쪼개지 않고 통째로 외우는 관용 덩어리

- **Diagnostic reference levels help identify unusually high doses.** — 진단참고수준 `C-MDX-008`
- **If a hospital is above the DRL, it should review its protocols.** — DRL 초과 시 `C-MDX-009`
- **For children, the protocols should be adjusted to body size.** — 소아 프로토콜 `C-MDX-010`
- **In interventional procedures, skin injuries can occur after long fluoroscopy times.** — 투시시간과 피부손상 `C-MDX-011`
- **Interventional cardiologists often have high lens doses.** — 중재 심장의 수정체 `C-MDX-012`
- **Lead glasses reduce the lens dose, but not as much as people think.** — 납안경 효과 한계 `C-MDX-013`
- **Ceiling-suspended shields are very effective if used properly.** — 천장 차폐 `C-MDX-014`
- **Many staff don't wear their dosimeters consistently.** — 착용 불량 `C-MDX-015`
- **We recommend two dosimeters — one under and one over the apron.** — 이중 선량계 `C-MDX-016`
- **Nuclear medicine staff get most of their dose from patients after injection.** — 핵의학 직원 선량원 `C-MDX-017`
- **Finger doses can be high when preparing radiopharmaceuticals.** — 손가락 선량 `C-MDX-018`
- **After radioiodine therapy, patients are given instructions to limit exposure to others.** — 방사성요오드 후 안내 `C-MDX-019`
- **The release criteria depend on the measured dose rate at one meter.** — 퇴원 기준 1m `C-MDX-020`
- **Every medical exposure must be justified.** — 정당화 `C-MDX-021`
- **Optimization means getting the image quality you need with the lowest dose.** — 의료 최적화 `C-MDX-022`
- **Patients often ask whether a scan will give them cancer.** — 환자 질문 `C-MDX-023`
- **The benefit of the right diagnosis usually far outweighs the small risk.** — 환자 답변 `C-MDX-024`
- **AI reconstruction allows lower-dose CT without losing image quality.** — AI 재구성 저선량 `C-MDX-025`

- ★ **Korean is agglutinative, so tokenization has a big effect on performance.** — 교착어 토큰화 `C-NLP-010`
- **We used a morphological analyzer before indexing.** — 형태소 분석 선행 `C-NLP-011`
- **Subword tokenizers handle new technical terms reasonably well.** — 서브워드 `C-NLP-012`
- **We built a named entity recognizer for nuclide names and dose units.** — 개체명 인식기 `C-NLP-013`
- **Relation extraction is harder — for example, linking a limit to the right group of people.** — 관계 추출 어려움 `C-NLP-014`
- **We classify incoming reports by topic automatically.** — 자동 분류 `C-NLP-015`
- **Automatic summaries are fluent, but they sometimes miss key numbers.** — 요약의 숫자 누락 `C-NLP-016`
- **Language models are still surprisingly bad at handling numbers and units.** — 숫자·단위 취약 `C-NLP-017`
- **We normalize all units to SI before indexing.** — SI 정규화 `C-NLP-018`
- **We built a synonym dictionary for terms like "effective dose" and its Korean equivalents.** — 동의어 사전 `C-NLP-019`
- **Expanding abbreviations improved recall noticeably.** — 약어 확장 효과 `C-NLP-020`
- **For some tasks, a simple regular expression beats a neural model.** — 정규식이 나을 때 `C-NLP-021`
- **Writing clear annotation guidelines took longer than the annotation itself.** — 지침 작성 부담 `C-NLP-022`
- **Inter-annotator agreement was 0.78 for entities.** — 개체 일치도 `C-NLP-023`
- **Domain-specific labeled data is always the bottleneck.** — 도메인 라벨 병목 `C-NLP-024`
- **We used weak supervision to create training labels.** — 약지도 학습 `C-NLP-025`
- **Active learning cut the annotation effort by about half.** — 능동학습 절감 `C-NLP-026`
- **Parallel Korean–English documents let us align terminology.** — 병렬 문서 정렬 `C-NLP-027`
- **Machine translation of technical text is good but not perfect.** — 기계번역 수준 `C-NLP-028`
- **The model translated "dose equivalent" as "equivalent dose," which is a different quantity.** — 번역 오류 예 `C-NLP-029`
- **We report precision, recall, and F1 at the entity level.** — 개체 단위 지표 `C-NLP-030`
- **Under lenient matching, F1 goes up to 0.9.** — 느슨한 매칭 `C-NLP-031`

*상황 32개 · ★ 2개*

**Z00 · (고정 표현 · chunk)** — 패턴으로 쪼개지 않고 통째로 외우는 관용 덩어리

- ★ **We separated retrieval evaluation from answer evaluation.** — 검색·답변 분리 평가 `C-LEV-010`
- **We froze the test set before tuning anything.** — 테스트셋 동결 `C-LEV-011`
- **There's a risk that public benchmarks are already in the training data.** — 벤치마크 오염 `C-LEV-012`
- **That's why we built our own domain benchmark.** — 자체 벤치 이유 `C-LEV-013`
- **Some questions have one correct answer; others need expert judgment.** — 정답 유형 혼재 `C-LEV-014`
- **We used a five-point rubric for correctness and completeness.** — 5점 루브릭 `C-LEV-015`
- **The experts didn't know which model produced each answer.** — 블라인드 `C-LEV-016`
- **We randomized the order to avoid position bias.** — 순서 무작위 `C-LEV-017`
- **Pairwise comparison was easier for the experts than absolute scoring.** — 쌍대비교 쉬움 `C-LEV-018`
- **Model A won 62 percent of the head-to-head comparisons.** — 승률 62% `C-LEV-019`
- **We bootstrapped confidence intervals for the win rates.** — 승률 부트스트랩 `C-LEV-020`
- **The LLM judge preferred longer answers, even when they were wrong.** — 긴 답 선호 편향 `C-LEV-021`
- **We calibrated the judge against a set of expert-labeled answers.** — 판정자 보정 `C-LEV-022`
- **We checked whether each citation actually supports the claim.** — 인용 정확도 `C-LEV-023`
- **We also measured how often the model correctly refused to answer.** — 올바른 거절 `C-LEV-024`
- **Some models refused too often, which is also a problem.** — 과잉 거절 `C-LEV-025`
- **We tested for dangerous advice, such as ignoring dose limits.** — 위험 조언 시험 `C-LEV-026`
- **We ran a small red-teaming exercise with our staff.** — 소규모 레드팀 `C-LEV-027`
- **We set the temperature to zero for reproducibility.** — 온도 0 `C-LEV-028`
- **We record the exact model version and quantization.** — 버전·양자화 기록 `C-LEV-029`
- **We also report performance per dollar.** — 달러당 성능 `C-LEV-030`
- **In the user study, people trusted answers with citations more.** — 인용 신뢰 `C-LEV-031`
- **We classified errors into retrieval failures, reasoning errors, and hallucinations.** — 오류 3분류 `C-LEV-032`

- **Our network is physically separated from the internet.** — 물리적 망분리 `C-INF-008`
- **Bringing a new model inside requires a security review.** — 모델 반입 심사 `C-INF-009`
- **The review usually takes about two weeks.** — 심사 2주 `C-INF-010`
- **We serve the models with vLLM.** — vLLM 서빙 `C-INF-011`
- **It handles about 50 concurrent users.** — 동시 50명 `C-INF-012`
- **Our target is under five seconds per answer.** — 5초 목표 `C-INF-013`
- **We use 4-bit quantization to fit the larger models.** — 4비트로 대형 모델 `C-INF-014`
- **We measured the quality loss from quantization — it was small.** — 양자화 손실 작음 `C-INF-015`
- **All queries are logged for auditing.** — 감사용 로그 `C-INF-016`
- **Personal data are masked before logging.** — 로그 마스킹 `C-INF-017`
- **Access is controlled by user role.** — 역할별 접근 `C-INF-018`
- **We update the models about twice a year.** — 연 2회 업데이트 `C-INF-019`
- **We keep the previous version so we can roll back.** — 롤백 대비 `C-INF-020`
- **We have a dashboard for latency and error rates.** — 모니터링 대시보드 `C-INF-021`
- **Power and cooling turned out to be a real constraint.** — 전력·냉각 제약 `C-INF-022`
- **The upfront cost is high, but running costs are low.** — 초기비 vs 운영비 `C-INF-023`
- **Compared with cloud APIs, it pays for itself in about two years.** — 2년 회수 `C-INF-024`
- **If the server goes down, the system falls back to keyword search.** — 키워드 검색 폴백 `C-INF-025`
- **We trained users on what the system can and can't do.** — 사용 범위 교육 `C-INF-026`

- **It depends on how reversible the action is.** — 되돌릴 수 있는지 `C-AGT-005`

**Z00 · (고정 표현 · chunk)** — 패턴으로 쪼개지 않고 통째로 외우는 관용 덩어리

- ★ **The agent can decide which tool to call — for example, a dose calculator or a document search.** — 에이전트 개념 `C-AGT-007`
- **Each tool has a clear description and input schema.** — 도구 스키마 `C-AGT-008`
- **Function-calling accuracy was about 85 percent on our test set.** — 함수호출 85% `C-AGT-009`
- **The most common error was calling the wrong tool.** — 잘못된 도구 선택 `C-AGT-010`
- **Sometimes the model passed the wrong units to the calculator.** — 단위 인자 오류 `C-AGT-011`
- **We added a validation step before any tool is executed.** — 실행 전 검증 `C-AGT-012`
- **We grade agents by autonomy level, from suggestion only to full execution.** — 자율성 등급 `C-AGT-013`
- **For now, we only allow level one — the human approves every action.** — 1등급만 허용 `C-AGT-014`
- **The agent has read-only access to the database.** — DB 읽기 전용 `C-AGT-015`
- **Every tool call is logged with its inputs and outputs.** — 도구 호출 로그 `C-AGT-016`
- **Errors compound quickly in multi-step tasks.** — 다단계 오류 누적 `C-AGT-017`
- **Asking the model to write a plan first improved reliability.** — 계획 먼저 `C-AGT-018`
- **We set a maximum number of steps to prevent loops.** — 최대 단계 제한 `C-AGT-019`
- **The agent asks a human when it's unsure.** — 불확실 시 질문 `C-AGT-020`
- **We evaluate agents on complete tasks, not single steps.** — 과제 단위 평가 `C-AGT-021`

- **How did you deal with mixed units in the old records?** — 구 단위 혼재 `C-DTP-003`

**F14 · Did you consider ___? / Have you tried ___?** — 대안 제시형 질문

- **Have you considered versioning the data with DVC?** — DVC 버전관리 `C-DTP-004`

**F19 · The thing is, ___** — 속사정·핵심 문제 제기

**Z00 · (고정 표현 · chunk)** — 패턴으로 쪼개지 않고 통째로 외우는 관용 덩어리

- ★ **The pipeline goes from raw records to a cleaned, versioned dataset.** — 파이프라인 개요 `C-DTP-006`
- **We defined a strict schema for every table.** — 엄격한 스키마 `C-DTP-007`
- **Each step has automatic checks — for example, no negative doses.** — 자동 점검 `C-DTP-008`
- **We removed duplicate records using worker ID and date.** — ID·날짜 중복 제거 `C-DTP-009`
- **Suspicious values are flagged, not deleted.** — 삭제 대신 표시 `C-DTP-010`
- **Older records were in rem, so we converted them to sieverts.** — rem → Sv `C-DTP-011`
- **One rem is ten millisieverts.** — 1 rem = 10 mSv `C-DTP-012`
- **Dates came in three different formats.** — 날짜 형식 3종 `C-DTP-013`
- **Job codes changed twice over the years, so we built a mapping table.** — 직무코드 매핑 `C-DTP-014`
- **Every dataset version has a hash, so results are traceable.** — 데이터 해시 `C-DTP-015`
- **Missing values are coded consistently as NA.** — NA 일관 표기 `C-DTP-016`
- **For the text data, we removed headers, footers, and page numbers.** — 머리말·꼬리말 제거 `C-DTP-017`
- **We split documents by section headings.** — 섹션 단위 분할 `C-DTP-018`
- **Tables were extracted separately and converted to Markdown.** — 표 별도 추출 `C-DTP-019`
- **Equations were the hardest part to extract cleanly.** — 수식 추출 난제 `C-DTP-020`
- **We wrote a data card describing the source, coverage, and known issues.** — 데이터 카드 `C-DTP-021`
- **For the scenario dataset, we standardized the format for supervised fine-tuning.** — 시나리오 SFT 표준화 `C-DTP-022`
- **Each example has an instruction, an input, and an expected output.** — SFT 형식 `C-DTP-023`
- **For DPO, we created pairs of preferred and rejected answers.** — DPO 쌍 `C-DTP-024`
- **Low-quality examples were filtered out by length and by expert review.** — 품질 필터 `C-DTP-025`

*상황 26개 · ★ 2개*


**F12 · It would be interesting to ___ / I wonder whether ___** — 제안·호기심

**F13 · How did you ___?** — 방법 질문

- ★ **How did you decide on the number of queries?** — 질의 수 결정 `C-EXD-002`
- **How did you randomize the assignment?** — 무작위 방법 `C-EXD-003`

**F14 · Did you consider ___? / Have you tried ___?** — 대안 제시형 질문

- **Did you run a pilot study first?** — 파일럿 여부 `C-EXD-004`

**F21 · Assuming (that) ___** — 가정 제시

- **Assuming the queries are representative, the results should generalize.** — 대표성 가정 `C-EXD-005`

**F22 · To what extent ___?** — 정도 질문

- **To what extent do the expert-written queries reflect real user needs?** — 전문가 질의 대표성 정도 `C-EXD-006`

**Z00 · (고정 표현 · chunk)** — 패턴으로 쪼개지 않고 통째로 외우는 관용 덩어리

- ★ **Our research question was whether smaller models are good enough.** — 연구 질문 `C-EXD-007`
- **Our hypothesis was that multilingual models would perform better on mixed-language text.** — 가설 `C-EXD-008`
- **The primary endpoint was nDCG at ten.** — 1차 평가변수 `C-EXD-009`
- **Secondary endpoints included latency and memory use.** — 2차 평가변수 `C-EXD-010`
- **We used BM25 as the control condition.** — BM25 대조 `C-EXD-011`
- **Queries were assigned to annotators at random.** — 주석자 무작위 배정 `C-EXD-012`
- **We stratified the queries by difficulty and language.** — 난이도·언어 층화 `C-EXD-013`
- **Each model was tested on exactly the same queries.** — 동일 질의 `C-EXD-014`
- **We planned the comparisons before looking at the results.** — 사전 비교 계획 `C-EXD-015`
- **We estimated that 300 queries per language would give enough precision.** — 언어별 300 `C-EXD-016`
- **We ran a small pilot first to check the procedure.** — 파일럿 선행 `C-EXD-017`
- **The pilot showed that some questions were ambiguous, so we rewrote them.** — 파일럿 후 수정 `C-EXD-018`
- **The annotators didn't know the purpose of the study.** — 목적 비공개 `C-EXD-019`
- **External validity is limited because the queries were written by experts.** — 외적 타당도 한계 `C-EXD-020`
- **We tried to rule out alternative explanations.** — 대안 설명 배제 `C-EXD-021`
- **Each model was run three times to check stability.** — 3회 반복 `C-EXD-022`
- **We used a factorial design: model by chunk size by language.** — 요인 설계 `C-EXD-023`
- **The interaction between model and language was significant.** — 모델×언어 상호작용 `C-EXD-024`
- **Most of the variance came from the queries, not the models.** — 질의가 분산 대부분 `C-EXD-025`
- **We limited the number of comparisons to keep the analysis focused.** — 비교 수 제한 `C-EXD-026`

### `C-UNC` 측정 불확도·계측학

*상황 27개 · ★ 2개*


**D04 · What does ___ mean? / What do you mean by ___?** — 의미 확인

- **Sorry, by k equals two you mean the coverage factor?** — k값 의미 `C-UNC-001`

**F05 · The results suggest ___ / It seems that ___** — 헤징 (단정 회피)

- **It seems the uncertainty is dominated by just one or two components.** — 1~2개 성분 지배 `C-UNC-002`

**F13 · How did you ___?** — 방법 질문

- ★ **How did you estimate the energy dependence component?** — 에너지 성분 추정 `C-UNC-003`
- **How did you handle correlated components?** — 상관 처리 `C-UNC-004`

**F14 · Did you consider ___? / Have you tried ___?** — 대안 제시형 질문

- **Have you tried Monte Carlo propagation?** — MC 전파 제안 `C-UNC-005`

**F21 · Assuming (that) ___** — 가정 제시

- **Assuming the components are independent, the combined uncertainty is about eight percent.** — 독립 가정 `C-UNC-006`

**G12 · To put this in context, ___** — 맥락화

- **To put this in context, an eight percent uncertainty is quite typical for personal dosimetry.** — 8% 맥락 `C-UNC-007`

**Z00 · (고정 표현 · chunk)** — 패턴으로 쪼개지 않고 통째로 외우는 관용 덩어리

- ★ **We followed the GUM approach for the uncertainty budget.** — GUM 접근 `C-UNC-008`
- **We combined Type A and Type B uncertainties.** — A형·B형 결합 `C-UNC-009`
- **The combined standard uncertainty was about eight percent.** — 합성 8% `C-UNC-010`
- **The expanded uncertainty with k equals 2 is about sixteen percent.** — 확장 k=2 `C-UNC-011`
- **That corresponds to roughly 95 percent coverage.** — 약 95% 포함 `C-UNC-012`
- **The largest component is the energy dependence.** — 최대 성분 에너지 `C-UNC-013`
- **Angular dependence adds another few percent.** — 방향 의존 성분 `C-UNC-014`
- **The calibration uncertainty is relatively small.** — 교정 성분 작음 `C-UNC-015`
- **Fading contributes about two percent.** — 페이딩 2% `C-UNC-016`
- **Some components are correlated, so we can't simply add them in quadrature.** — 상관 성분 `C-UNC-017`
- **We also propagated the uncertainties with Monte Carlo sampling.** — MC 전파 `C-UNC-018`
- **The Monte Carlo result agreed with the GUM estimate.** — GUM·MC 일치 `C-UNC-019`
- **Our calibration is traceable to the national standard.** — 국가표준 소급 `C-UNC-020`
- **The reference instruments are recalibrated every two years.** — 2년 재교정 `C-UNC-021`
- **We define the detection limit following ISO 11929.** — ISO 11929 `C-UNC-022`
- **The decision threshold is lower than the detection limit.** — 결정 문턱 `C-UNC-023`
- **At low doses, the relative uncertainty becomes very large.** — 저선량 상대불확도 `C-UNC-024`
- **The trumpet curve shows the acceptable range at each dose level.** — 트럼펫 곡선 `C-UNC-025`
- **Our results fall inside the trumpet curve.** — 트럼펫 내 결과 `C-UNC-026`
- **We always report the dose with its uncertainty.** — 불확도 동반 보고 `C-UNC-027`

- **How do you validate software changes?** — 소프트웨어 변경 검증 `C-QAS-003`

- ★ **Our dosimetry lab is accredited to ISO/IEC 17025.** — ISO 17025 인정 `C-QAS-006`
- **The scope covers photon and beta dosimetry.** — 인정 범위 `C-QAS-007`
- **We do an internal audit every year.** — 연 1회 내부감사 `C-QAS-008`
- **The accreditation body visits every two years.** — 2년마다 외부평가 `C-QAS-009`
- **They found one minor nonconformity.** — 경미 부적합 1건 `C-QAS-010`
- **We fixed it within a month.** — 한 달 내 시정 `C-QAS-011`
- **Each nonconformity needs a corrective action plan.** — 시정조치 계획 `C-QAS-012`
- **We did a root cause analysis.** — 근본원인 분석 `C-QAS-013`
- **Every procedure is documented and version-controlled.** — 절차서 버전관리 `C-QAS-014`
- **Training records are kept for every staff member.** — 교육 기록 `C-QAS-015`
- **We take part in proficiency tests every year.** — 숙련도 시험 참가 `C-QAS-016`
- **Our results were within the acceptable range.** — 숙련도 적합 `C-QAS-017`
- **Any change to the dose algorithm has to be validated before use.** — 알고리즘 변경 검증 `C-QAS-018`
- **We have a formal change control process.** — 변경관리 절차 `C-QAS-019`
- **Documents need two signatures before release.** — 이중 서명 `C-QAS-020`
- **Dose records must be kept for the worker's lifetime, or at least 30 years after.** — 선량기록 보존 `C-QAS-021`
- **During the audit, we had to show the full calculation for a random sample.** — 무작위 표본 계산 시연 `C-QAS-022`
- **That's why every constant in our software is visible and traceable.** — 상수 추적성 `C-QAS-023`

- **The detector needs a few minutes to stabilize.** — 검출기 안정화 `C-LAB-016`

### `C-CAL` 교정 실무 대화

*상황 20개 · ★ 2개*


**F13 · How did you ___?** — 방법 질문

**F21 · Assuming (that) ___** — 가정 제시

**Z00 · (고정 표현 · chunk)** — 패턴으로 쪼개지 않고 통째로 외우는 관용 덩어리

- ★ **The calibration is scheduled for next Tuesday.** — 교정 일정 `C-CAL-005`
- **We'll set up the reference field at two meters from the source.** — 기준장 설정 `C-CAL-006`
- **The air kerma rate at the reference point is 1.2 milligray per hour.** — 공기커마율 `C-CAL-007`
- **Place the dosimeters on the front of the slab phantom.** — 팬텀 위 배치 `C-CAL-008`
- **We need an exposure time of about 40 minutes for 1 millisievert.** — 조사 시간 계산 `C-CAL-009`

- **The certificate needs the lab manager's signature.** — 성적서 서명 `C-CAL-020`

### `C-SFC` 안전문화·인적요소

*상황 24개 · ★ 2개*


**F13 · How did you ___?** — 방법 질문

- ★ **How do you encourage people to report near misses?** — 근접사고 보고 독려 `C-SFC-001`

**F14 · Did you consider ___? / Have you tried ___?** — 대안 제시형 질문

- **Did you compare doses between contractors and permanent staff?** — 협력사·정규직 비교 `C-SFC-002`

**F16 · In our case, ___** — 자기 맥락 비교

- **In our plants, the dose per job has dropped since we introduced pre-job briefings.** — 브리핑 효과 `C-SFC-003`

**F19 · The thing is, ___** — 속사정·핵심 문제 제기

- **The thing is, culture changes slowly — much more slowly than technology.** — 문화는 느리게 `C-SFC-004`

**Z00 · (고정 표현 · chunk)** — 패턴으로 쪼개지 않고 통째로 외우는 관용 덩어리

- ★ **Safety culture is about what people do when nobody's watching.** — 안전문화 정의 `C-SFC-005`
- **We want people to report near misses without fear of blame.** — 두려움 없는 보고 `C-SFC-006`
- **A no-blame approach encourages honest reporting.** — 비난 없는 접근 `C-SFC-007`
- **Most of our learning comes from near misses, not accidents.** — 근접사고에서 배움 `C-SFC-008`
- **Human error is usually a symptom of a system problem.** — 인적오류는 증상 `C-SFC-009`
- **People sometimes skip steps when procedures are too long.** — 긴 절차 생략 `C-SFC-010`
- **We simplified the procedures, and compliance went up.** — 절차 단순화 효과 `C-SFC-011`
- **We hold a pre-job briefing before every high-dose task.** — 작업 전 브리핑 `C-SFC-012`
- **After the job, we compare the actual dose with the plan.** — 작업 후 비교 `C-SFC-013`
- **Each team sets its own dose target.** — 팀별 선량 목표 `C-SFC-014`
- **We recognize teams that achieve their dose targets.** — 목표 달성 포상 `C-SFC-015`
- **Fatigue is a big factor during long outages.** — 정비 기간 피로 `C-SFC-016`
- **Shift handovers are a common point of failure.** — 교대 인수인계 `C-SFC-017`
- **We use three-way communication for critical instructions.** — 3-way 소통 `C-SFC-018`
- **Managers have to lead by example.** — 솔선수범 `C-SFC-019`
- **Contractor workers often receive higher doses than permanent staff.** — 협력사 선량 `C-SFC-020`
- **We give the same training to contractors as to our own staff.** — 협력사 동일 교육 `C-SFC-021`
- **With AI tools, there's a new risk: people trusting the output without thinking.** — AI 맹신 위험 `C-SFC-022`
- **We run a safety culture survey every two years.** — 2년마다 설문 `C-SFC-023`
- **The results showed that younger staff are less willing to speak up.** — 젊은 직원 침묵 `C-SFC-024`

### `C-END` 내분비 심화 대화

*상황 31개 · ★ 2개*


**D04 · What does ___ mean? / What do you mean by ___?** — 의미 확인

**F05 · The results suggest ___ / It seems that ___** — 헤징 (단정 회피)

**F12 · It would be interesting to ___ / I wonder whether ___** — 제안·호기심

**F13 · How did you ___?** — 방법 질문

- ★ **How did you evaluate the closed-loop algorithm?** — 폐회로 평가 `C-END-004`
- **How accurate are the CGM readings at low glucose levels?** — 저혈당 구간 CGM 정확도 `C-END-005`

**F14 · Did you consider ___? / Have you tried ___?** — 대안 제시형 질문

- **Did you look at glucose patterns in shift workers?** — 교대근무 혈당 패턴 `C-END-006`

**F16 · In our case, ___** — 자기 맥락 비교

- **In Korea, about one in seven adults has diabetes.** — 한국 당뇨 유병률 `C-END-007`

**F22 · To what extent ___?** — 정도 질문

- **To what extent does shift work explain the higher HbA1c?** — 교대근무 설명 정도 `C-END-008`

**Z00 · (고정 표현 · chunk)** — 패턴으로 쪼개지 않고 통째로 외우는 관용 덩어리

- ★ **Most thyroid nodules are benign.** — 결절 대부분 양성 `C-END-009`
- **We do a fine-needle aspiration if the nodule looks suspicious on ultrasound.** — 세침흡인 `C-END-010`
- **We use the TI-RADS system to classify nodules.** — TI-RADS `C-END-011`
- **Graves' disease is the most common cause of hyperthyroidism.** — 그레이브스 `C-END-012`
- **Radioiodine is a good option for patients who don't respond to medication.** — 방사성요오드 적응증 `C-END-013`
- **After radioiodine, many patients need lifelong thyroid hormone.** — 평생 호르몬 `C-END-014`
- **Type 1 diabetes is autoimmune; type 2 is mainly about insulin resistance.** — 1형·2형 차이 `C-END-015`
- **Insulin pumps combined with CGM are becoming standard for type 1.** — 펌프+CGM 표준화 `C-END-016`
- **Closed-loop systems adjust insulin automatically.** — 폐회로 `C-END-017`
- **The algorithm predicts glucose and adjusts the basal rate every few minutes.** — 폐회로 알고리즘 `C-END-018`
- **GLP-1 receptor agonists have changed how we treat type 2 diabetes.** — GLP-1 변화 `C-END-019`
- **They also lead to significant weight loss.** — 체중 감소 `C-END-020`
- **SGLT2 inhibitors protect the heart and kidneys.** — SGLT2 장기보호 `C-END-021`
- **Diabetic retinopathy is still a leading cause of blindness.** — 망막병증 `C-END-022`
- **AI screening for retinopathy works very well now.** — AI 망막 선별 `C-END-023`
- **Osteoporosis is underdiagnosed in men.** — 남성 골다공증 `C-END-024`
- **Adrenal incidentalomas are often found on CT scans done for other reasons.** — 부신 우연종 `C-END-025`
- **Pituitary tumors can cause a wide range of hormone problems.** — 뇌하수체 종양 `C-END-026`
- **Gestational diabetes is increasing in Korea.** — 임신성 당뇨 증가 `C-END-027`
- **Metabolic syndrome is very common in shift workers.** — 교대근무 대사증후군 `C-END-028`
- **We're looking at metabolic risk in nuclear plant shift workers.** — 원전 교대근무 연구 `C-END-029`
- **Remote glucose monitoring lets us adjust treatment between visits.** — 원격 혈당 관리 `C-END-030`
- **Patient education is still the most important part of diabetes care.** — 환자 교육 핵심 `C-END-031`

### `C-STA` 통계 자문 요청·응대

*상황 20개 · ★ 1개*


**Z00 · (고정 표현 · chunk)** — 패턴으로 쪼개지 않고 통째로 외우는 관용 덩어리

- ★ **Could I get your advice on the statistical analysis?** — 자문 요청 `C-STA-001`
- **We have repeated measurements from the same workers over ten years.** — 반복측정 상황 `C-STA-002`
- **What's the best way to account for that?** — 처리 방법 질문 `C-STA-003`
- **A mixed-effects model with a random intercept for each worker would be a good start.** — 혼합모형 권고 `C-STA-004`
- **Alternatively, you could use generalized estimating equations.** — GEE 대안 `C-STA-005`
- **The difference is whether you're interested in individual or population-level effects.** — 개인 vs 집단 수준 `C-STA-006`
- **How many participants would we need to detect a 10 percent difference?** — 표본 크기 질문 `C-STA-007`
- **It depends on the variance — do you have pilot data?** — 분산에 달림 `C-STA-008`
- **We're testing twenty outcomes — do we need to correct for that?** — 20개 결과 보정 `C-STA-009`
- **Yes, or at least pre-specify a primary outcome.** — 1차 결과 사전 지정 `C-STA-010`
- **The residuals aren't normal — is that a problem?** — 비정규 잔차 `C-STA-011`
- **With this sample size, it's probably fine, but a log transform might help.** — 로그 변환 조언 `C-STA-012`
- **About 15 percent of the data are missing — what should we do?** — 결측 15% `C-STA-013`
- **Multiple imputation is usually better than dropping incomplete cases.** — 다중대체 권고 `C-STA-014`
- **How should we interpret an interaction term?** — 상호작용 해석 `C-STA-015`
- **What's the clearest way to show these results?** — 표현 방법 `C-STA-016`
- **Would you use R or Python for this?** — R vs Python `C-STA-017`
- **That's really helpful — thank you.** — 자문 감사 `C-STA-018`
- **We'd be happy to include you as a co-author if you're involved in the analysis.** — 공저 제안 `C-STA-019`
- **I can give general advice, but you'd need a proper statistician for the final analysis.** — 자문 한계 `C-STA-020`

- ★ **Radiation is energy that travels through space — some of it, like sunlight, we see every day.** — 방사선이란 `C-EXL-001`
- **We're all exposed to natural radiation from the ground, the air, and even our food.** — 자연방사선 일상 `C-EXL-002`
- **Bananas contain a tiny bit of radioactive potassium — it's completely natural.** — 바나나 비유 `C-EXL-003`
- **We measure the effect of radiation in sieverts — usually in thousandths of a sievert.** — 선량 단위 쉽게 `C-EXL-004`
- **As with medicine, it's the dose that matters.** — 양이 독을 만든다 `C-EXL-005`
- **Workers wear a small badge that records how much radiation they've received.** — 선량계 쉽게 `C-EXL-006`
- **The reactor is surrounded by thick concrete and steel to keep radiation inside.** — 원전 안전 쉽게 `C-EXL-007`
- **There are three ways to reduce exposure: less time, more distance, and shielding.** — 거리·시간·차폐 `C-EXL-008`
- **A long flight gives you more radiation than living next to a nuclear plant for a year.** — 비행 비교 쉽게 `C-EXL-009`
- **A chest X-ray is a very small dose — about the same as a few days of natural background.** — X선 비교 `C-EXL-010`
- **Being exposed to radiation doesn't make you radioactive.** — 오염 vs 피폭 `C-EXL-011`
- **Iodine tablets only protect the thyroid, and only if taken at the right time.** — 요오드 쉽게 `C-EXL-012`
- **It's like sunlight — a little is fine, but you wouldn't stay in the sun all day without protection.** — 아이 질문 답 `C-EXL-013`
- **It's completely normal to worry about radiation — let me explain what we know.** — 걱정 공감 `C-EXL-014`
- **There are some things scientists still debate, especially at very low doses.** — 모르는 것 인정 (대중) `C-EXL-015`
- **There's no such thing as a silly question about this.** — 질문 환영 (대중) `C-EXL-016`

*상황 25개 · ★ 2개*


**F13 · How did you ___?** — 방법 질문

**F14 · Did you consider ___? / Have you tried ___?** — 대안 제시형 질문

**Z00 · (고정 표현 · chunk)** — 패턴으로 쪼개지 않고 통째로 외우는 관용 덩어리

- ★ **We use a portable survey meter for dose rate checks.** — 서베이미터 `C-DET-004`
- **Contamination monitors are at every exit from the controlled area.** — 오염 감시기 `C-DET-005`
- **The whole-body contamination monitor takes about ten seconds.** — 전신 오염 감시 `C-DET-006`
- **A Geiger counter is sensitive, but it doesn't give accurate dose rates at all energies.** — GM 계수관 `C-DET-007`
- **Ion chambers are more accurate for dose rate measurements.** — 이온전리함 `C-DET-008`
- **Scintillation detectors are good for identifying nuclides.** — 섬광 검출기 `C-DET-009`
- **For high-resolution spectroscopy, we use HPGe detectors.** — HPGe `C-DET-010`
- **HPGe detectors need to be cooled, usually with liquid nitrogen or an electric cooler.** — 냉각 필요 `C-DET-011`
- **Neutron monitors are bulky because of the moderator.** — 중성자 측정기 `C-DET-012`
- **The electronic dosimeter alarms if the dose rate exceeds the set limit.** — 전자선량계 경보 `C-DET-013`
- **We set the alarm at 1 millisievert per hour for most jobs.** — 경보 설정값 `C-DET-014`
- **The instrument takes a few seconds to respond to a change.** — 응답 시간 `C-DET-015`
- **At very high count rates, dead time becomes a problem.** — 불감시간 `C-DET-016`
- **We check every survey meter with a check source each morning.** — 기기 점검 (일일) `C-DET-017`
- **Always check the battery before entering the area.** — 배터리 점검 `C-DET-018`
- **Hold the meter at about one meter from the surface for the dose rate.** — 측정 위치 `C-DET-019`
- **For contamination, keep the probe about one centimeter from the surface.** — 오염 측정 거리 `C-DET-020`
- **Make sure the meter is showing microsieverts per hour, not counts per second.** — 측정 단위 혼동 `C-DET-021`
- **We convert counts per second to becquerels using the efficiency.** — cps → Bq `C-DET-022`
- **Detector efficiency depends on the energy and the geometry.** — 효율 교정 `C-DET-023`
- **If the reading looks too stable, the detector might be faulty.** — 기기 고장 판단 `C-DET-024`
- **Always bring a backup meter for high-dose work.** — 예비 기기 `C-DET-025`

### `C-NPP` 원전 운영·정비 맥락 설명

*상황 24개 · ★ 2개*


**F05 · The results suggest ___ / It seems that ___** — 헤징 (단정 회피)

- **It seems the water chemistry changes reduced dose rates by about 20 percent.** — 수화학 효과 헤징 `C-NPP-001`

**F13 · How did you ___?** — 방법 질문

- ★ **How do you predict the dose for an outage before it starts?** — 정비 선량 예측 `C-NPP-002`

**F16 · In our case, ___** — 자기 맥락 비교

- **In Korea, outages happen roughly every 18 months.** — 국내 정비 주기 `C-NPP-003`

**G12 · To put this in context, ___** — 맥락화

- **To put this in context, a single outage can account for 70 percent of a plant's annual collective dose.** — 정비 선량 맥락 `C-NPP-004`

**Z00 · (고정 표현 · chunk)** — 패턴으로 쪼개지 않고 통째로 외우는 관용 덩어리

- ★ **An outage is when the reactor is shut down for maintenance and refueling.** — 계획예방정비 설명 `C-NPP-005`
- **An outage usually takes about one to two months.** — 정비 기간 `C-NPP-006`
- **During an outage, the number of workers on site can triple.** — 정비 중 인력 `C-NPP-007`
- **Most of the annual collective dose is received during outages.** — 선량 집중 `C-NPP-008`
- **Most Korean reactors are pressurized water reactors.** — 가압경수로 `C-NPP-009`
- **We also have a few CANDU reactors, which have different dose issues, especially tritium.** — 중수로 `C-NPP-010`
- **The steam generators are one of the main sources of exposure.** — 증기발생기 `C-NPP-011`
- **Radiation fields come mainly from activated corrosion products in the coolant system.** — 원자로 냉각재 계통 `C-NPP-012`
- **Cobalt-60 is the dominant nuclide in most work areas.** — Co-60 주 핵종 `C-NPP-013`
- **Water chemistry control helps reduce the buildup of radioactive deposits.** — 수화학 관리 `C-NPP-014`
- **Chemical decontamination of the system can reduce dose rates significantly.** — 제염 (계통) `C-NPP-015`
- **Workers need a special permit to enter the radiation controlled area.** — 방사선 관리구역 `C-NPP-016`
- **Every job in the controlled area needs a radiation work permit.** — 작업허가 `C-NPP-017`
- **High-dose jobs are reviewed by an ALARA committee in advance.** — ALARA 검토 위원회 `C-NPP-018`
- **For complex jobs, workers practice on a full-scale mock-up first.** — 목업 훈련 `C-NPP-019`
- **We use cameras and remote dosimetry to monitor workers in real time.** — 원격 감시 `C-NPP-020`
- **Decommissioning brings a whole new set of radiation protection challenges.** — 해체 `C-NPP-021`
- **During decommissioning, the dose is spread over many more workers and years.** — 해체 선량 `C-NPP-022`
- **Several reactors are going through license renewal for long-term operation.** — 수명 연장 `C-NPP-023`
- **New reactors are designed with lower occupational doses in mind.** — 신규 원전 `C-NPP-024`

### `C-PRN` ICRP 방호 체계 토론

*상황 20개 · ★ 1개*


**F11 · I see what you mean, but ___** — 정중한 반대

- **I see the argument, but optimization at low doses still makes sense for workers with high cumulative doses.** — 과잉 최적화 이견 `C-PRN-001`

**F12 · It would be interesting to ___ / I wonder whether ___** — 제안·호기심

- **It would be interesting to see how AI tools could support optimization decisions.** — AI의 최적화 지원 `C-PRN-002`

**F22 · To what extent ___?** — 정도 질문

- **To what extent is optimization really applied below 1 millisievert?** — 원칙 적용 정도 `C-PRN-003`

**Z00 · (고정 표현 · chunk)** — 패턴으로 쪼개지 않고 통째로 외우는 관용 덩어리

- ★ **The system is based on three principles: justification, optimization, and dose limitation.** — 3대 원칙 `C-PRN-004`
- **Justification means the benefit should outweigh the harm.** — 정당화 설명 `C-PRN-005`
- **Optimization means keeping doses as low as reasonably achievable, taking social and economic factors into account.** — 최적화 설명 `C-PRN-006`
- **Dose limits apply only to planned exposure situations.** — 한도 설명 `C-PRN-007`
- **ICRP distinguishes planned, emergency, and existing exposure situations.** — 피폭 상황 3분류 `C-PRN-008`
- **Radon in homes is an example of an existing exposure situation.** — 현존 피폭 `C-PRN-009`
- **Dose constraints are a tool for optimization, not limits.** — 선량 제약치 `C-PRN-010`
- **Reference levels play a similar role in emergency and existing situations.** — 참조준위 설명 `C-PRN-011`
- **We also distinguish occupational, public, and medical exposure.** — 피폭 범주 `C-PRN-012`
- **There are no dose limits for patients — only justification and optimization.** — 의료 피폭 한도 없음 `C-PRN-013`
- **"Reasonably achievable" is always open to interpretation.** — 합리적 달성 논쟁 `C-PRN-014`
- **Some argue that optimization has gone too far at very low doses.** — 최적화 과잉 `C-PRN-015`
- **Stakeholder involvement is now a key part of optimization.** — 이해관계자 참여 `C-PRN-016`
- **ICRP Publication 138 describes the ethical values behind the system.** — 윤리적 기반 `C-PRN-017`
- **ICRP is currently reviewing the system, and new general recommendations are expected.** — ICRP 개정 진행 `C-PRN-018`
- **Topics include the detriment calculation and the role of effective dose.** — 개정 주제 `C-PRN-019`
- **The protection of the environment is also part of the system now.** — 비인간 생물 `C-PRN-020`

- **Satisfaction was 4.2 out of 5.** — 만족도 지표 `C-KPI-008`

- **Maybe we need to revise the indicators — some aren't very meaningful.** — 지표 재검토 `C-KPI-013`
- **I've put all the indicators on a shared dashboard.** — 대시보드 공유 `C-KPI-014`

- **Our main safety KPI is collective dose per outage.** — 선량 KPI `C-KPI-016`
- **We're also looking at leading indicators, like the number of pre-job briefings.** — 선행 지표 `C-KPI-017`
- **Lagging indicators only tell you what already went wrong.** — 후행 지표 한계 `C-KPI-018`
- **We compare our indicators with the WANO averages.** — 벤치마킹 (지표) `C-KPI-019`
- **Let's add one indicator for data quality.** — 지표 개선 제안 `C-KPI-020`

*상황 20개 · ★ 1개*

- ★ **Our security rules are very strict because we're part of a nuclear utility.** — 보안 규정 `C-CYB-003`
- **USB drives aren't allowed on the internal network.** — USB 금지 `C-CYB-004`
- **All incoming attachments are scanned and sometimes delayed.** — 이메일 첨부 검사 `C-CYB-005`
- **We can't use external cloud services for any work data.** — 외부 클라우드 금지 `C-CYB-006`
- **Moving files between networks requires a formal request.** — 망간 자료 전송 `C-CYB-007`
- **A file transfer can take a day or two to be approved.** — 전송 소요 `C-CYB-008`
- **We have regular phishing exercises.** — 피싱 훈련 `C-CYB-009`
- **This email looks suspicious — I'll report it to IT.** — 피싱 의심 메일 `C-CYB-010`
- **Passwords have to be changed every three months.** — 비밀번호 정책 `C-CYB-011`
- **Remote access requires two-factor authentication.** — 2단계 인증 `C-CYB-012`
- **We use a clean laptop for overseas trips.** — 출장 노트북 규정 `C-CYB-013`
- **I avoid logging in to anything sensitive on public Wi-Fi.** — 공용 와이파이 주의 `C-CYB-014`
- **Even open-source models have to be checked for hidden risks.** — 모델 공급망 `C-CYB-015`
- **We verify the checksums of every model file we bring in.** — 모델 서명 검증 `C-CYB-016`
- **All access to the AI system is logged and reviewed.** — 로그 감사 (보안) `C-CYB-017`
- **Prompt injection is a new kind of risk we have to think about.** — 프롬프트 인젝션 `C-CYB-018`
- **The system ignores instructions hidden inside retrieved documents.** — 문서 내 지시 차단 `C-CYB-019`
- **There's always a trade-off between security and convenience.** — 보안 vs 편의 `C-CYB-020`

**Z00 · (고정 표현 · chunk)** — 패턴으로 쪼개지 않고 통째로 외우는 관용 덩어리

- ★ **The same English term is translated three different ways in Korean documents.** — 용어 불일치 문제 `C-TRM-005`
- **We use the official Korean translation of the ICRP publications as the reference.** — 공식 번역 우선 `C-TRM-006`
- **We built a bilingual glossary with about 800 terms.** — 용어집 구축 `C-TRM-007`
- **Each term in the glossary has a definition and a source.** — 정의 동반 `C-TRM-008`
- **"Dose equivalent" and "equivalent dose" are often confused, even by experts.** — 혼동 쌍 예 `C-TRM-009`
- **Equivalent dose is a protection quantity; dose equivalent is an operational one.** — 혼동 쌍 설명 `C-TRM-010`
- **Some terms are just transliterated into Korean, which makes search harder.** — 외래어 표기 `C-TRM-011`
- **Many Korean documents keep English abbreviations like ALARA or TLD.** — 약어 그대로 `C-TRM-012`
- **Every translated document is checked by two experts.** — 번역 검수 절차 `C-TRM-013`
- **We use machine translation first, then post-edit.** — 기계번역 후 편집 `C-TRM-014`
- **Post-editing saves about 40 percent of the time.** — 사후편집 시간 절감 `C-TRM-015`
- **We force the translation tool to use our glossary terms.** — 용어 강제 기능 `C-TRM-016`
- **We also cross-check with the IAEA safety glossary.** — IAEA 용어집 참조 `C-TRM-017`
- **Even between English-speaking countries, some terms are used differently.** — 국가 간 차이 `C-TRM-018`
- **It would help if ICRP published official term lists in several languages.** — 용어 표준 제안 `C-TRM-019`

- **Our version matched the vendor output for all 200 test cases.** — 재구성 검증 `C-RPR-013`
- **We found a small difference in how they handle readings below background.** — 차이 발견 `C-RPR-014`
- **The difference affects less than one percent of records.** — 차이 영향 `C-RPR-015`
- **We publish everything — code, inputs, and expected outputs.** — 공개 원칙 `C-RPR-016`
- **The reproduction package runs on a standard laptop.** — 재현 패키지 `C-RPR-017`
- **We rate each result as fully, partly, or not reproducible.** — 재현성 등급 `C-RPR-018`

## 부록 A. Vol.3 핵심(★) 압축 목록

### Part 1. 공통 의사소통 확장

**FVR 부탁하기 — 크고 작은 부탁** — *Is there any way you could fit me in today?*

**TIM 시간·약속 표현** — *How often do the trains run at night?* · *I'm a bit early — I'll wait outside.*

**DSR 묘사하기 — 사람·물건·장소** — *It's a navy backpack with a laptop sleeve and a small Korean flag patch.*

**CPR 비교·선택 말하기** — *This one's cheaper, but that one looks better made.*

**PLN 계획·의도·결정** — *I can't wait to see the Philharmonic.* · *I'm going to visit the Reichstag tomorrow morning.*

**CRT 확신·추측의 정도** — *I'm absolutely sure.*

**HAB 습관·빈도·익숙함** — *I'm used to spicy food.* · *I usually go for a walk before breakfast.*

**TXT 문자·메신저 구어** — *Here!*

**PLT 공손도 조절 — 같은 뜻, 다른 온도** — *Can you shut the window?*

**PRB 문제 해결 대화 일반** — *I've got a bit of a problem.*

**BRG 일상 협상·조율** — *Let's meet in the middle.*

**NUX 숫자 실전 말하기** — *That comes to 34.80.*

**ADR 주소·위치 말하기** — *How far is it from the station?* · *It's Friedrichstraße 43, second floor.*

**ENC 격려·칭찬 주고받기** — *You did a fantastic job.*

**JOK 유머·가벼운 대화** — *My German is about as good as my singing — not great.*

**QUE 줄 서기·대기** — *Is this the end of the line?*

**INR 말 순서·끼어들기·발언권** — *Sorry, can I just say something?*

**CHK 이해 확인·재확인 심화** — *So, if I've understood correctly, we need to pay first and then collect the ticket?*

**BOD 몸 상태·감각 표현** — *I'm starving.*

**ASR 요청에 답하기** — *Sure, no problem at all.*

**ADV 제안·조언하기 (일상)** — *Maybe you could try asking at the front desk.*

**DCL 거절·사양 (일상)** — *I'm fine, thanks — I just ate.*

**GRE 인사 변형·시간대별 인사** — *Good morning — how are you today?*

### Part 2. 여행 확장

**MIL 항공 마일리지·라운지·우수회원** — *I don't think my miles from the last flight were credited.*

**HSL 호스텔·게스트하우스·공용 숙소** — *Hi, I've got a bed booked in the six-bed dorm.*

**BFT 아침 식사·브런치 주문** — *Could I get two fried eggs, sunny side up?*

**DIE 식이 요구 상세 대화** — *Is there any risk of cross-contamination?* · *I'm vegetarian — no meat or fish, but eggs and dairy are fine.*

**SWT 디저트·케이크·베이커리** — *A slice of the cheesecake, please.*

**PHM 약사 상담 심화** — *What would you recommend for this?* · *Hi, I'd like some advice about my symptoms.*

**SCN 경치·도시 풍경 감상** — *What a view!*

**GDE 가이드 투어 참여 대화** — *Is this the meeting point for the 10 o'clock walking tour?*

**HIS 역사·사회 화제 대화** — *Where were you when the wall came down?*

**TST 음식 맛·식감 묘사** — *It's really rich and full of flavor.*

**ANN 안내방송·표지 이해** — *Did they just say the train is cancelled?*

**FRA 인접국 체류 생존** — *Bonjour! Sorry, do you speak English?*

**KFK 카페 문화·커피 대화** — *Is "Kaffee und Kuchen" an afternoon tradition here?*

**SOU 기념품·선물 고르기 상세** — *I'm looking for something small to bring back for my team — about twenty people.*

**JET 여행 중 컨디션 관리** — *I'm trying to stay awake until ten to beat the jet lag.*

**BKS 서점·문구점·도서관** — *I'm looking for the English books section.*

**WCF 화장실·공공 편의시설** — *Where are the toilets in the station?*

**DMT 만성질환·당뇨 여행 관리** — *I need to keep my insulin in my carry-on — it can't go in the hold.*

**SPC 좌석·공간 양해** — *Here, I'll move my bag.*

**PAY 결제 심화** — *Card, please.*

**LST 길 잃었을 때 심화** — *I'm completely lost.*

**WLK 도보 여행 자체 기획** — *I'm planning to walk from Mitte to Kreuzberg.*

**SHW 쇼핑몰·백화점** — *Which floor is menswear on?*

**SPO 스포츠·야외 활동 참여** — *Can you rent kayaks on the river?*

**NTR 야간열차·침대칸** — *I'd like to book a sleeper cabin to Vienna.*

**LTS 장기 체류 일상** — *I'd like to know your weekly rate.*

**MTU 밋업·언어교환** — *Are there any English-speaking meetups in Berlin?*

**BTH 온천·스파·수영장 (Therme)** — *Is there a good thermal spa near Berlin?*

**HAI 미용실·이발 상세** — *I'd like to book a haircut for tomorrow afternoon.*

**RCP 호텔 프런트 잡담·체류 중 요청** — *Morning! Any recommendations for today?*

**CRP 카풀·차량 공유** — *Is there a ride-sharing app for long distances here?*

**PRT 파티·사교 모임** — *Hi! Thanks for inviting me.*

**WRK 여행 중 업무** — *Is there a desk in the room where I could work?*

**EMB 대사관·영사 서비스** — *What are the opening hours of the consular section?*

**ECO 친환경·재사용 실천** — *I brought my own cup — could you use this?*

**ART 공방·체험 클래스** — *Are there any pottery classes for beginners?*

**CKG 요리 교실·시장 투어** — *Are there any German cooking classes for visitors?*

**PCK 짐 싸기·출발 전 점검** — *Let me go through my checklist one more time.*

**DLY 지연 대기 중 다른 승객과 대화** — *Looks like we're all going to be here for a while.*

**TIP 팁·서비스 문화 대화** — *What's a normal tip in a restaurant here — five or ten percent?*

**ACC 접근성·이동 약자 동행** — *Is the venue fully wheelchair accessible?*

**SNO 겨울 여행·크리스마스 마켓** — *When do the Christmas markets open this year?*

**ROA 로드트립·고속도로 휴게소** — *What time should we leave to avoid traffic?*

**OVB 숙소 체크인 문제 심화** — *I booked a double room, but this is a twin.*

**ORD 온라인 주문·호텔 배송·반품** — *Can I have an online order delivered to my hotel?*

**NOI 소음·이웃 갈등 (숙소)** — *Sorry to bother you, but could you keep the music down a bit? It's quite late.*

**AGN 여행 상담·투어 예약 창구** — *Hi, I've got two free days and I'd like some ideas.*

**TXP 여행 경험 나누기** — *The highlight was definitely the concert at the Philharmonic.*

### Part 3. 학회·연구 활동 확장

**BIO 방사선 생물학적 영향·역학** — *How did you reconstruct the doses for the early years?* · *To put this in context, the lifetime cancer risk from 1 millisievert is about one in 20,000.* · *The atomic bomb survivor cohort is still the main basis for risk estimates.*

**MCS 몬테카를로·계산 선량평가** — *Which variance reduction techniques did you use?* · *Assuming the worker is facing the source, the dose would be about 20 percent higher.* · *We used MCNP6 for the transport calculations.*

**IPX 내부피폭 심화** — *How did you handle the unknown intake date?* · *We used the ICRP biokinetic models for cesium and cobalt.*

**ENV 환경방사선·감시·공간분석** — *How did you set the alarm threshold?* · *We monitor dose rates at about 40 points around each site.*

**EMG 비상대응·사고 관리** — *How do you measure whether an exercise was successful?* · *The emergency planning zones extend to about 30 kilometers.*

**MDX 의료방사선·환자선량** — *How did you set the local DRLs?* · *We report CTDIvol and DLP for each scan.*

**NLP 자연어처리 일반** — *How did you train the annotators?* · *Korean is agglutinative, so tokenization has a big effect on performance.*

**LEV LLM 평가 심화** — *How did you develop the scoring rubric?* · *We separated retrieval evaluation from answer evaluation.*

**INF 온프렘 인프라·배포** — *How do you handle security reviews for new models?* · *We have two servers with four GPUs each.*

**AGT 에이전트·도구 사용** — *How do you stop the agent from doing something unsafe?* · *The agent can decide which tool to call — for example, a dose calculator or a document search.*

**DTP 데이터 파이프라인·전처리** — *How did you handle duplicate records?* · *The pipeline goes from raw records to a cleaned, versioned dataset.*

**EXD 실험·연구 설계** — *How did you decide on the number of queries?* · *Our research question was whether smaller models are good enough.*

**UNC 측정 불확도·계측학** — *How did you estimate the energy dependence component?* · *We followed the GUM approach for the uncertainty budget.*

**QAS 품질보증·인정·감사** — *How are the proficiency tests organized?* · *Our dosimetry lab is accredited to ISO/IEC 17025.*

**LAB 실험실·측정실 작업 대화** — *Let the reader warm up for 30 minutes before we start.*

**CAL 교정 실무 대화** — *How do you decide on the calibration interval?* · *The calibration is scheduled for next Tuesday.*

**ISP 규제 검사·현장점검 대응** — *Let me get back to you on the exact training dates — I'll check the records.* · *The regulator will inspect the dosimetry service next month.*

**ITV 채용 면접·방문연구 지원 인터뷰** — *I'm a senior researcher with about 25 years of experience in health physics and data science.*

**CWR 공동 집필·문서 협업** — *Let me get back to you on the statistics question — I need to check with our statistician.* · *Where are we with the discussion section?* · *Could you take the methods, and I'll do the introduction?*

**CRI 비판적 토론·가정 검증** — *To what extent does this depend on the choice of test set?*

**RPT 보고서·기술문서 문장** — *This report summarizes the results of the 2026 benchmark study.*

**STY 발표 스토리텔링·고급 전환** — *To put this in context, our staff search these documents about a thousand times a month.*

**DNR 만찬 화제 심화** — *Do you know much about German wine? I'm told the Rieslings are excellent.*

**HST 학회·워크숍 주최자 입장** — *On behalf of the organizing committee, welcome to Seoul.*

**LGS 학회 운영 문의 (참가자)** — *Is it possible to get an invitation letter on letterhead, with my name and the dates?*

**TPR 귀국 후 결과 공유 (파트너와)** — *Just a quick note to say thanks again for your hospitality.* · *Thank you all for the productive discussions in Berlin.*

**THS 학위논문 심사·디펜스** — *Thank you, committee members, for being here today.*

**SFC 안전문화·인적요소** — *How do you encourage people to report near misses?* · *Safety culture is about what people do when nobody's watching.*

**END 내분비 심화 대화** — *How did you evaluate the closed-loop algorithm?* · *Most thyroid nodules are benign.*

**PRG 프로그래밍·구현 대화** — *How do you make sure the JavaScript and Python versions agree?* · *We wrote the core engine in Python and the interface in JavaScript.*

**GEN 업무용 생성형 AI 활용 논의** — *How does your institute regulate AI use?* · *We use AI mainly for literature search and first drafts.*

**STA 통계 자문 요청·응대** — *Could I get your advice on the statistical analysis?*

**ACD 학계·출판 문화 대화** — *The pressure to publish is getting worse everywhere.*

**SEM 세미나·초청강연 청중과 초청** — *Would you be willing to give a seminar at our institute when you're in Korea?*

**BUD 예산·구매·조달 대화** — *We need to request budget for two more GPUs next year.*

**HRD 인력·조직 관리 대화** — *Where are we with the quarterly report?* · *Let's divide the work so nobody is overloaded.*

**EXL 일반인 대상 방사선 설명** — *Radiation is energy that travels through space — some of it, like sunlight, we see every day.*

**VFB 그래프·시각화 피드백** — *The y-axis needs a label and units.*

**SRC 문헌 검색·연구 도구 사용** — *Let's combine "occupational exposure" with "lens opacity" in PubMed.*

**NRV 발표 불안·자기관리 대화** — *I always get nervous before presenting in English.*

**BOS 상사·지도교수와 대화 (보고·요청)** — *Let me get back to you on the exact costs by tomorrow.* · *I wanted to give you a quick update on the benchmark project.*

**IDE 연구 아이디어 브레인스토밍** — *Let's just throw ideas out — no judgment for now.*

**PAT 특허·IP·기술이전** — *Do you think this algorithm could be patented?*

**AGD 학회 중 동료와 일정 조율** — *Which sessions are you going to this afternoon?*

**HOS 적대적·공격적 질문 대응 심화** — *I appreciate your strong interest in this.*

**STQ 학생 질문 응대 (교수·멘토 입장)** — *That's a really good question — let's think it through together.*

**KOM 신규 협력 킥오프 회의** — *Where are we with the partner contact list?* · *Welcome, everyone, to the kick-off meeting for our joint project.*

**DET 방사선 계측기 일반** — *How do you choose which instrument to use for each job?* · *We use a portable survey meter for dose rate checks.*

**NPP 원전 운영·정비 맥락 설명** — *How do you predict the dose for an outage before it starts?* · *An outage is when the reactor is shut down for maintenance and refueling.*

**PRN ICRP 방호 체계 토론** — *The system is based on three principles: justification, optimization, and dose limitation.*

**KPI 성과지표·평가 대화** — *What should be our main indicator of success?*

**CYB 정보보안·사이버** — *Our security rules are very strict because we're part of a nuclear utility.*

**TRM 번역·용어 표준화 토론** — *When you say "exposure," do you mean the quantity in roentgen or exposure in general?* · *The same English term is translated three different ways in Korean documents.*

**RPR 재현·검증 연구 대화** — *What environment did you use for the original results?* · *We tried to reproduce the results from the original paper.*

**AWD 수상·시상식** — *Congratulations — that was well deserved!*

**PRS 연구 홍보·뉴스레터 글** — *Small AI Models Help Radiation Workers Find Answers Faster*

## 부록 C. 중복 검증 기록과 근사 고지

### C.1 중복 검증 결과

| 항목 | 값 |
|---|---|
| Vol.3 원천 작성 문장 | 3040 |
| Vol.1·2와 정규화 일치로 제외 | 6 |
| Vol.3 내부 중복으로 제외 | 0 |
| **Vol.3 최종 채택** | **3034** |
| Vol.3 카테고리 / ★ | 127 / 165 |
| Vol.1·2 상황 수 (정규화 고유) | 6111 (6045) |
| 통합 상황 수 (Vol.1~3) | 9145 |
| 통합 정규화 고유 표현 | 9079 |
| Vol.3 ∩ Vol.1·2 (정규화) | 0 (assert 통과) |
| Vol.3 내부 중복 (정규화) | 0 (assert 통과) |
| 전체 ID 중복 | 0 (assert 통과) |
| 기존 카테고리 코드 충돌 | 0 (assert 통과) |

빌드 과정에서 카테고리 코드 충돌이 한 건 발견되었다. Vol.3의 일반인 설명 카테고리를 처음에 C-LAY로 작성했는데, Vol.2의 T-LAY(장시간 경유)와 코드가 같았다. 이 카테고리는 C-EXL로 변경했다. 통합 상황 수와 고유 표현 수의 차이는 Vol.1 내부에서 장소만 다르게 같은 문장을 재사용한 경우(예: *Can I pay by card?*)에서 나온다.

### C.2 ID와 통합 데이터

통합 CSV의 `volume` 열은 1·2·3 중 하나의 값을 가진다. 패턴 CSV의 `added_in` 열은 각 패턴이 추가된 권을 표시한다. `count_vol1_2`와 `count_vol3` 열은 Vol.1·2 합계와 Vol.3 수록 수를 따로 기록한다. 통합 JSON의 `meta`에는 권별 상황 수와 통합 고유 수가 들어 있다. Vol.1·Vol.2의 ID는 이전 배포본과 동일하다.

### C.3 근사·한계 고지

`[ADD]` 모든 표현은 모델이 생성한 것이며 원어민 검수를 거치지 않았다. 발표·서면·면접 문장은 실제로 쓰기 전에 원어민 동료에게 확인받기를 권한다.

`[ADD]` 아래 항목은 모두 문형 학습용 가상 예시이며, 실제 연구 결과를 가리키지 않는다.
- Part 3의 수치: 승률 62 %, 함수호출 85 %, 합성 불확도 8 %, 만족도 4.2 등
- 인명: Dr. Weber, Markus, Jonas 등
- 금액·일정: 1억 2천만 원, 6개월 등
- 홍보 문구 속 인용문

`[DOC]` 아래 항목은 일반적으로 알려진 사실을 소재로 썼다.
- 방사선 생물·역학: 명목 위험계수 약 5 %/Sv, DDREF 2, 백내장 역치 약 0.5 Gy
- 계측·환산: 1 rem = 10 mSv, GUM, ISO 11929, ISO/IEC 17025
- 방호 체계·문헌: ICRP 3대 원칙과 피폭 상황 3분류, ICRP 103/110/116/138
- 국내 현황: 한국 원전 전력 비중 약 3분의 1, 성인 당뇨 유병률 약 7명 중 1명
- 여행 제도: 솅겐·스위스 로밍·영국 ETA

시점에 따라 바뀌는 수치(유병률, 원전 비중, ETA·ETIAS 시행)는 발언하기 전에 최신 공식 자료로 확인해야 한다.

`[ADD]` 아래 문장은 의사소통 연습용이며, 처치 지침이나 개인 의료 조언이 아니다.
- 만성질환·당뇨 여행 관리(T-DMT)
- 약사 상담(T-PHM)
- 내분비 대화(C-END)

`[ADD]` 중복 판정은 문자열 정규화 기준이다. 뜻은 같지만 표현이 다른 문장은 중복으로 보지 않았다. 다만 작성 단계에서 장면이나 기능이 겹치지 않도록 구분했다.
